# Surface d=3 NZ direct QClifford data-residual Hoare derivation ledger
# Compact notation, no hidden Lean theorem discharge in the listed X data proof.
# Fat predicates are derived below from the smaller assertion core.
# circuit instructions=112; gates=44; errLocs=68; local Pauli side branches=204.
# global branches with <3 faults: 1 no-fault + 204 one-fault + 20502 two-fault = 20707.
# checker=true; bad residual leaves=0; proof size=348; expanded size=552; depth=117.
# HONEST SCOPE: the 112-line trace below is still the closed logical-X data-residual proof.
# New in the Lean code: betaZ/LogicalZ/LogicalAny syntax, the H-And certificate rule,
# and the explicit LogicalAny-from-XZ consequence rule are now present and build.
# Remaining unclosed proof payload: the dual betaZ barrier contract and 204-branch betaZ trace.
# It does not yet prove the stronger syndrome-record/decoder circuit-level FT statement.

SURFACE-D3 CONCRETE DATA
No named barrier/logical-class carrier is part of this proof notation.
distance := 3
layout := data(qi)=ambient qi for i=0..8; q9=syndrome ancilla
DIST_DATA_X := LogicalX(data[]) -> 3 <= faults[]
DIST_DATA_Z := LogicalZ(data[]) -> 3 <= faults[]
DIST_DATA_ANY := LogicalAny(data[]) -> 3 <= faults[]
BI_X := 3 <= betaX(data[]) + faults[]
BI_Z := 3 <= betaZ(data[]) + faults[]
BI_PAIR := BI_X /\ BI_Z
DIST_DATA_ANY is derived from DIST_DATA_X /\ DIST_DATA_Z by E-LogicalAnyFromXZ.
The closed DIST_DATA_ANY certificate still awaits the dual betaZ trace/contract.
DIST_FULL is not derived here; it must mention every measured syndrome bit and the decoder correction.
S[s0] = {q0=Z*q1=Z*q3=Z*q4=Z}
S[s1] = {q1=X*q2=X*q4=X*q5=X}
S[s2] = {q3=X*q4=X*q6=X*q7=X}
S[s3] = {q4=Z*q5=Z*q7=Z*q8=Z}
S[s4] = {q0=X*q1=X}
S[s5] = {q2=Z*q5=Z}
S[s6] = {q3=Z*q6=Z}
S[s7] = {q7=X*q8=X}
logicalZ = {q0=Z*q1=Z*q2=Z}
logicalX = {q0=X*q3=X*q6=X}
Hook[s0] = [{q1=Z*q3=Z*q4=Z}, {q1=Z*q4=Z}, {q4=Z}] + [{q0=Z*q1=Z*q3=Z*q4=Z}]
Hook[s1] = [{q2=X*q4=X*q5=X}, {q4=X*q5=X}, {q5=X}] + [{q1=X*q2=X*q4=X*q5=X}]
Hook[s2] = [{q4=X*q6=X*q7=X}, {q6=X*q7=X}, {q7=X}] + [{q3=X*q4=X*q6=X*q7=X}]
Hook[s3] = [{q5=Z*q7=Z*q8=Z}, {q5=Z*q8=Z}, {q8=Z}] + [{q4=Z*q5=Z*q7=Z*q8=Z}]
Hook[s4] = [{q1=X}] + [{q0=X*q1=X}]
Hook[s5] = [{q5=Z}] + [{q2=Z*q5=Z}]
Hook[s6] = [{q6=Z}] + [{q3=Z*q6=Z}]
Hook[s7] = [{q8=X}] + [{q7=X*q8=X}]

CORE ASSERTION SYNTAX
ops ::= [] | Gate(g)::ops | Inj(q,P)::ops
inst ::= g | !q | Inj(q,P)
S ::= [] | inst;S
E ::= I | {q=P * ...} | data[ops] | E*E | update(E,q,P) | S[s]
M ::= flip[ops,q]
Bool ::= true | false | parity(E,E) | M
N ::= n | faults[ops] | wt(E) | N+N | N-N | count{i in finite-set : B(i)} | min_{x in finite-set} N(x)
B ::= Bool=Bool | clean[ops] | E=E | N<=N | N<N | B/\B | B\/B | B->B | not B | forall x:T. B | exists x:T. B
clean[ops] := data[ops]=I /\ faults[ops]=0.
The Lean concrete clean assertion is stronger: the whole QClifford state after ops equals QCState.clean.
BI_X expands to: 3 <= betaX(data[]) + faults[]
BI_Z expands to: 3 <= betaZ(data[]) + faults[]
DIST_DATA_X expands to: LogicalX(data[]) -> 3 <= faults[]
DIST_DATA_Z expands to: LogicalZ(data[]) -> 3 <= faults[]
DIST_DATA_ANY expands to: LogicalAny(data[]) -> 3 <= faults[]
flip[ops,q] observes the current measurement-flip bit for physical qubit q after ops.
pre[S](B) means B must hold after first executing the concrete instruction list S.
pre is not implication and not arithmetic order; it is a notation macro expanded below.
pre[[]](B) := B.
pre[g;S](B) prepends Gate(g) to every dynamic observer in pre[S](B).
pre[Inj(q,P);S](B) prepends Inj(q,P) to every dynamic observer in pre[S](B).
pre[!q;S](B) is derived below as pre[S](B) /\ AllP(q,S,B).
pre[InjResidual(R);S](B) is proof-only notation: first multiply the current data residual by R, spend one fault, then continue with S.
Proof lines use pre[S](B); the old order-like WP chain notation is no longer used.
The arrow -> occurs only inside object-level logical implication B->B.
The symbol ==> is meta-level entailment between assertions/rules.
Tbl(q,[S],B; X:=R:uX; Y:=R:uY; Z:=R:uZ) is a derived assertion, expanded below.
use ::= id | data(q,P) | hook[s]; defer is rejected and does not occur.

DERIVED SURFACE-D3 PREDICATES
parity(A,E)=0 abbreviates parity(A,E)=false; parity(A,E)=1 abbreviates parity(A,E)=true.
Centralizer(E) := parity(S[s0],E)=0 /\ parity(S[s1],E)=0 /\ parity(S[s2],E)=0 /\ parity(S[s3],E)=0 /\ parity(S[s4],E)=0 /\ parity(S[s5],E)=0 /\ parity(S[s6],E)=0 /\ parity(S[s7],E)=0.
LogicalX(E) := Centralizer(E) /\ parity(logicalZ,E)=1.
LogicalZ(E) := Centralizer(E) /\ parity(logicalX,E)=1.
LogicalAny(E) := Centralizer(E) /\ (parity(logicalZ,E)=1 \/ parity(logicalX,E)=1).
This is the concrete Surface-d3 instance of shared LogicalSetSymbol:
  surfaceD3_anyLogical.parityZero = [S[s0],S[s1],S[s2],S[s3],S[s4],S[s5],S[s6],S[s7]]
  surfaceD3_anyLogical.parityAny = [logicalZ, logicalX]
  surfaceD3_anyLogical.distance = 3
For one encoded qubit, LogicalAny(E) is equivalent to Centralizer(E) /\ not Stab(E); the syntactic assertion uses the finite parityAny disjunction above.
Masks(0) := [[]].
Masks(n+1) := map(false::_, Masks(n)) ++ map(true::_, Masks(n)).
Prod([]) := I.
Prod(b::bs at index s) := if b then S[s]*Prod(bs) else Prod(bs).
Stab(E) := OR_{m in Masks(8)} E = Prod(m).
Group(q,i) := (i=0 /\ q in {q0,q1,q2}) \/ (i=1 /\ q in {q3,q4,q5}) \/ (i=2 /\ q in {q6,q7,q8}).
ColGroup(q,i) := (i=0 /\ q in {q0,q3,q6}) \/ (i=1 /\ q in {q1,q4,q7}) \/ (i=2 /\ q in {q2,q5,q8}).
HookPred[s0](E) := E={q1=Z*q3=Z*q4=Z} \/ E={q1=Z*q4=Z} \/ E={q4=Z} \/ E=S[s0].
HookPred[s1](E) := E={q2=X*q4=X*q5=X} \/ E={q4=X*q5=X} \/ E={q5=X} \/ E=S[s1].
HookPred[s2](E) := E={q4=X*q6=X*q7=X} \/ E={q6=X*q7=X} \/ E={q7=X} \/ E=S[s2].
HookPred[s3](E) := E={q5=Z*q7=Z*q8=Z} \/ E={q5=Z*q8=Z} \/ E={q8=Z} \/ E=S[s3].
HookPred[s4](E) := E={q1=X} \/ E=S[s4].
HookPred[s5](E) := E={q5=Z} \/ E=S[s5].
HookPred[s6](E) := E={q6=Z} \/ E=S[s6].
HookPred[s7](E) := E={q8=X} \/ E=S[s7].
AllP(q,S,B) := pre[Inj(q,X);S](B) /\ pre[Inj(q,Y);S](B) /\ pre[Inj(q,Z);S](B).
pre[!q;S](B) := pre[S](B) /\ AllP(q,S,B).
betaX(E) := 3 - min_{m in Masks(8)} groupsX(Prod(m),E).
groupsX(S,E) := |{ i in {0,1,2} : exists q, Group(q,i) /\ hasX((S*E)[q]) }|.
betaX is X-support only.  It proves the logical-X/barZ direction and ignores a pure logical-Z residual.
betaZ(E) := 3 - min_{m in Masks(8)} groupsZ(Prod(m),E).
groupsZ(S,E) := |{ i in {0,1,2} : exists q, ColGroup(q,i) /\ hasZ((S*E)[q]) }|.
betaZ is Z-support only.  It proves the logical-Z/barX direction and ignores a pure logical-X residual.
The full data-logical proof uses BI_PAIR := BI_X /\ BI_Z plus E-LogicalAnyFromXZ.
Every Tbl row uses a concrete remaining-instruction list S and a postcondition B.
gates([]) := [].
gates(g::S) := Gate(g)::gates(S), when g is a deterministic gate.
gates(!q::S) := gates(S).
Res(q,P,S,B,R) := data[Inj(q,P)::gates(S)] = R*data[gates(S)] /\ faults[Inj(q,P)::gates(S)] = faults[gates(S)]+1.
UseOK(id,R) := R=I.
UseOK(data(q,P),R) := R={q=P}.
UseOK(hook[s],R) := HookPred[s](R).
LocalBI_X(R) := forall E:Err, forall f:Nat. 3 <= betaX(E)+f -> 3 <= betaX(R*E)+(f+1).
LocalBI_Z(R) := forall E:Err, forall f:Nat. 3 <= betaZ(E)+f -> 3 <= betaZ(R*E)+(f+1).
E-LocalBIXLift: LocalBI_X(R) /\ pre[S](BI_X) ==> pre[InjResidual(R);S](BI_X).
E-LocalBIZLift: LocalBI_Z(R) /\ pre[S](BI_Z) ==> pre[InjResidual(R);S](BI_Z).
Case(q,P,S,BI_X,R,u) := Res(q,P,S,BI_X,R) /\ UseOK(u,R) /\ LocalBI_X(R) /\ (Res(q,P,S,BI_X,R) /\ UseOK(u,R) /\ LocalBI_X(R) -> pre[Inj(q,P);S](BI_X)).
Case(q,P,S,BI_Z,R,u) := Res(q,P,S,BI_Z,R) /\ UseOK(u,R) /\ LocalBI_Z(R) /\ (Res(q,P,S,BI_Z,R) /\ UseOK(u,R) /\ LocalBI_Z(R) -> pre[Inj(q,P);S](BI_Z)).
Case(q,P,S,B,R,u) for non-BI_X postconditions has the same shape with the explicit final implication to pre[Inj(q,P);S](B).
Tbl(q,[S],B; X:=RX:uX; Y:=RY:uY; Z:=RZ:uZ) := pre[S](B) /\ Case(q,X,S,B,RX,uX) /\ Case(q,Y,S,B,RY,uY) /\ Case(q,Z,S,B,RZ,uZ).

ALLOWED HOARE / CHECKER RULES
H-Nil: {{ B }} [] {{ B }}
H-Gate: {{ pre[g;S](B) }} g {{ pre[S](B) }}
H-Err: {{ pre[!q;S](B) }} !q {{ pre[S](B) }}
E-TblElim: Tbl(q,[S],B; X:=RX:uX; Y:=RY:uY; Z:=RZ:uZ) ==> pre[!q;S](B)
H-ErrTable: {{ pre[!q;S](B) }} !q {{ pre[S](B) }} with explicit side premise Tbl(q,[S],B; X:=RX:uX; Y:=RY:uY; Z:=RZ:uZ)
Line grammar: {{ pre[!q;S](B) }} H-ErrTable !q using Tbl(q,[S],B; X:=RX:uX; Y:=RY:uY; Z:=RZ:uZ) {{ pre[S](B) }}
H-Seq: {{ A }} c1 {{ M }}, {{ M }} c2 {{ B }}  ==>  {{ A }} c1;c2 {{ B }}
H-And: {{ A1 }} C {{ B1 }}, {{ A2 }} C {{ B2 }}  ==>  {{ A1 /\ A2 }} C {{ B1 /\ B2 }}
H-Conseq: A ==> A', {{ A' }} c {{ B' }}, B' ==> B  ==>  {{ A }} c {{ B }}
E-Refl: A ==> A
E-Trans: A ==> B, B ==> C  ==>  A ==> C
E-AndIntro/Left/Right, E-ImpIntro/Elim, E-LeRefl, E-LeTrans
E-BIInit: clean[] ==> BI_X, from direct recursive betaX(I)=3
E-BIXDataToDistance: BI_X ==> DIST_DATA_X, from direct recursive LogicalX(E) -> betaX(E)=0
E-BIZInit: clean[] ==> BI_Z, from direct recursive betaZ(I)=3
E-BIZDataToDistance: BI_Z ==> DIST_DATA_Z, from direct recursive LogicalZ(E) -> betaZ(E)=0
E-CleanAnd: clean[] ==> clean[] /\ clean[]
E-LogicalAnyFromXZ: DIST_DATA_X /\ DIST_DATA_Z ==> DIST_DATA_ANY, from the explicit parity definitions of LogicalAny, LogicalX, and LogicalZ
H-LogicalAnyJoin:
  {{ clean[] }} C {{ DIST_DATA_X }},
  {{ clean[] }} C {{ DIST_DATA_Z }}
  ==>
  {{ clean[] }} C {{ DIST_DATA_ANY }}
  by H-Conseq(E-CleanAnd, H-And, E-LogicalAnyFromXZ)
E-LeafCheckToCase_X: LeafCheck_X(q,P,S,R,u) ==> Case(q,P,S,BI_X,R,u)
E-LeafCheckToCase_Z: LeafCheck_Z(q,P,S,R,u) ==> Case(q,P,S,BI_Z,R,u)
E-ResidualToConcreteInject_X: Res(q,P,S,BI_X,R) /\ pre[InjResidual(R);S](BI_X) ==> pre[Inj(q,P);S](BI_X)
E-ResidualToConcreteInject_Z: Res(q,P,S,BI_Z,R) /\ pre[InjResidual(R);S](BI_Z) ==> pre[Inj(q,P);S](BI_Z)
E-LocalBIXLift: LocalBI_X(R) /\ pre[S](BI_X) ==> pre[InjResidual(R);S](BI_X)
E-LocalBIZLift: LocalBI_Z(R) /\ pre[S](BI_Z) ==> pre[InjResidual(R);S](BI_Z)
H-CheckedTrace_X: if every concrete err table leaf checks Res/UseOK/LocalBI_X, {{ clean[] }} checkedTrace_X {{ pre[C](BI_X) }}
H-CheckedTrace_Z: if every concrete err table leaf checks Res/UseOK/LocalBI_Z, {{ clean[] }} checkedTrace_Z {{ pre[C](BI_Z) }}
LeafCheck_X(q,P,S,R,u): Res(q,P,S,BI_X,R) /\ UseOK(u,R) /\ LocalBI_X(R)
LeafCheck_Z(q,P,S,R,u): Res(q,P,S,BI_Z,R) /\ UseOK(u,R) /\ LocalBI_Z(R)
Residual part: R = residualAfterSuffix(layout,S,q,P)
Use-id: residual = I
Use-data(q,P): residual = update(I,q,P)
Use-hook[s]: HookPred[s](residual)
Pres-id: LocalBI_X follows by arithmetic from betaX(E) <= betaX(I*E)+1
Pres-data(q,P): LocalBI_X follows from the barrier triangle law and wt({q=P})=1
Pres-hook[s]: LocalBI_X follows from the concrete hook/alignment law for HookPred[s]
Pres-id-Z: LocalBI_Z follows by arithmetic from betaZ(E) <= betaZ(I*E)+1
Pres-data-Z(q,P): LocalBI_Z follows from the betaZ barrier triangle law and wt({q=P})=1
Pres-hook-Z[s]: LocalBI_Z must be supplied by the dual concrete hook/alignment law for HookPred[s]
Align-Gate: alignment proof recurses through a concrete gate
Align-Err: alignment proof carries exactly X/Y/Z leaves for one concrete err location

WHAT THE 204 LOCAL BRANCHES MEAN
The number 204 is not the number of global fault executions.
204 = 68 err locations * 3 non-identity Pauli choices.
Each H-ErrTable line carries its three rows as a Tbl side premise, so residuals such as {q0=X}
are assertion-language facts, not comments attached to the rule.
Each local leaf proves a suffix-residual preservation obligation valid for any incoming data error E and any incoming fault count f:
  BI_X(E,f) /\ local residual R with use id/data/hook  ==>  BI_X(R*E,f+1).
For a gate, propagation is deterministic and only rewrites the observers in BI_X.
Therefore the 112-line WP derivation composes these local obligations through the whole circuit.
This composition covers every global assignment theta : ErrLoc -> {I,X,Y,Z}, not just one fault.

EXPLICIT TWO-FAULT CONSEQUENCE
Let wt(theta) be the number of non-I choices in theta.
For every global fault assignment theta, the Hoare derivation gives:
  {{ clean[] }} C[theta] {{ 3 <= betaX(data_final(theta)) + wt(theta) }}.
The final rule expands to:
  LogicalX(data_final(theta)) -> betaX(data_final(theta)) = 0.
So for every theta with wt(theta) < 3:
  if LogicalX(data_final(theta)), then 3 <= wt(theta), contradiction.
In particular, all two-fault executions are covered:
  #two-fault branches = choose(68,2) * 3 * 3 = 2278 * 9 = 20502.
Together with 1 no-fault and 204 one-fault branches, this proves no logical-X data-residual failure for all 20707 branches with <3 faults.
The proof does not enumerate those 20707 branches; it derives them syntactically from nested pre expansion plus BI_X.

MEASUREMENT-RECORD GAP
Branches such as !q9 immediately before MeasZ(q9) can have data residual I while still flipping the
measurement outcome: X and Y before MeasZ toggle the measured bit. The current BI_X and DIST_DATA_X do
not mention that bit, so those branches are not certified as full syndrome-record branches here.
The target assertion syntax has flip[ops,q], but the current QClifford state stores only one flip
bit per physical qubit. Because q9 is reused for eight stabilizer measurements, flip[ops,q9] is only
an accumulated parity and cannot distinguish "s0 flipped" from "s7 flipped".
Therefore a full reused-ancilla circuit-level proof needs a smaller concrete record syntax, for
example rec[ops,k] for measurement event k, an explicit MeasZ(q,k) Hoare rule that stores that
event bit, plus a decoder relation corr(rec[]) and the postcondition:
  DIST_FULL := LogicalAny(corr(rec[])*data[]) -> 3 <= faults[]
or equivalently: for faults[] < 3, the decoded final data error is not logical.
That record-aware proof is not present in this ledger.

FINAL RULE GAP
H-DataXBarrierToDistance is transparent for the logical-X data statement:
  BI_X and LogicalX(data[]) imply betaX(data[])=0, hence 3 <= faults[].
It does not prove the logical-Z data statement.  The pure residual logicalZ itself has
parity(logicalZ,logicalZ)=0 and is deliberately outside LogicalX.
To prove DIST_DATA_ANY, add the dual column/Z-spread barrier certificate or a joint
LogicalAny barrier certificate, then derive LogicalAny(data[]) -> 3 <= faults[].
It is not a decoder theorem. It does not define the measured syndrome, the correction chosen by a
decoder, or how hook residuals and measurement-bit faults combine after correction. Those ingredients
must appear syntactically before claiming the full circuit-level FT theorem.

HOARE DERIVATION
ENTRY. {{ clean[] }} H-CheckedTrace localBranches=204 global<3=20707 check=true {{ pre[!q9; Prep0(q9); !q0; !q9; CX(q0,q9); !q3; !q9; CX(q3,q9); !q1; !q9; CX(q1,q9); !q4; !q9; CX(q4,q9); !q9; MeasZ(q9); !q9; PrepP(q9); !q9; !q1; CX(q9,q1); !q9; !q2; CX(q9,q2); !q9; !q4; CX(q9,q4); !q9; !q5; CX(q9,q5); !q9; H(q9); !q9; MeasZ(q9); !q9; PrepP(q9); !q9; !q3; CX(q9,q3); !q9; !q4; CX(q9,q4); !q9; !q6; CX(q9,q6); !q9; !q7; CX(q9,q7); !q9; H(q9); !q9; MeasZ(q9); !q9; Prep0(q9); !q4; !q9; CX(q4,q9); !q7; !q9; CX(q7,q9); !q5; !q9; CX(q5,q9); !q8; !q9; CX(q8,q9); !q9; MeasZ(q9); !q9; PrepP(q9); !q9; !q0; CX(q9,q0); !q9; !q1; CX(q9,q1); !q9; H(q9); !q9; MeasZ(q9); !q9; Prep0(q9); !q2; !q9; CX(q2,q9); !q5; !q9; CX(q5,q9); !q9; MeasZ(q9); !q9; Prep0(q9); !q3; !q9; CX(q3,q9); !q6; !q9; CX(q6,q9); !q9; MeasZ(q9); !q9; PrepP(q9); !q9; !q7; CX(q9,q7); !q9; !q8; CX(q9,q8); !q9; H(q9); !q9; MeasZ(q9)](BI_X) }}
